LICR\u586b\u8865\u7684\u7a7a\u767d\n\n```\n\u5f53\u524d\u6846\u67b6\u80fd\u529b\u8fb9\u754c\n\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502                                                             \u2502\n\u2502   \u6587\u672c\uff1a\"\u8bbe\u221a2 = p/q\uff0cp\u548cq\u4e92\u8d28...\"                            \u2502\n\u2502                                                             \u2502\n\u2502   LlamaIndex/LangChain/GraphRAG \u80fd\u62bd\u53d6\uff1a                     \u2502\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 \u221a2 (mathematical constant)                           \u2502\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 p, q (variables)                                     \u2502\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 \u4e92\u8d28 (coprime) \u2190 \u5982\u679c\u5728\u6587\u672c\u4e2d\u51fa\u73b0                     \u2502\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 \u65e0\u7406\u6570 (irrational number) \u2190 \u5982\u679c\u5728\u6587\u672c\u4e2d\u51fa\u73b0         \u2502\n\u2502                                                             \u2502\n\u2502   LICR \u989d\u5916\u80fd\u62bd\u53d6\uff1a                                          \u2502\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 proof by contradiction (Q184899) \u2190 \u9690\u6027\u65b9\u6cd5\u8bba         \u2502\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 reductio ad absurdum \u2190 \u540c\u4e49\u6982\u5ff5                       \u2502\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 infinite descent \u2190 \u6df1\u5c42\u4f9d\u8d56                          \u2502\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 fundamental theorem of arithmetic \u2190 \u7406\u8bba\u57fa\u7840          \u2502\n\u2502                                                             \u2502\n\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n```\n\n**\u6838\u5fc3\u5dee\u5f02**\uff1a\n\n| \u7ef4\u5ea6 | \u73b0\u6709\u6846\u67b6 | LICR |\n|------|---------|------|\n| \u62bd\u53d6\u76ee\u6807 | \u6587\u672c\u4e2d\u51fa\u73b0\u7684\u5b9e\u4f53 | \u6587\u672c\u4f53\u73b0\u7684\u6982\u5ff5 |\n| \u8bc6\u522b\u673a\u5236 | NER + \u5173\u7cfb\u62bd\u53d6 | \u8bed\u4e49\u6a21\u5f0f\u8bc6\u522b |\n| KB\u94fe\u63a5 | \u5b57\u7b26\u4e32\u5339\u914d | \u6982\u5ff5\u63a8\u65ad + API\u9a8c\u8bc1 |\n| \u8986\u76d6\u8303\u56f4 | \u663e\u5f0f\u63d0\u53ca | \u663e\u5f0f + \u9690\u5f0f |\n\n---\n\n## 3. \u96c6\u6210\u67b6\u6784\u8bbe\u8ba1\n\n### 3.1 \u4f5c\u4e3aLlamaIndex Extractor\u96c6\u6210\n\n```python\n# \u63d0\u8bae\u7684LICR Extractor\u5b9e\u73b0\nfrom llama_index.core.indices.property_graph import BaseLLMPathExtractor\nfrom typing import List, Tuple, Any\n\nclass LICRPathExtractor(BaseLLMPathExtractor):\n    \"\"\"\n    LICR (LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition) Extractor\n    \n    \u8bc6\u522b\u6587\u672c\u4f53\u73b0\u4f46\u672a\u663e\u5f0f\u547d\u540d\u7684\u9690\u6027\u6982\u5ff5\uff0c\u5e76\u94fe\u63a5\u5230Wikidata\u3002\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        llm,\n        wikidata_api_client,\n        concept_types: List[str] = [\"methodology\", \"domain_concept\", \"principle\"],\n        max_concepts_per_chunk: int = 10,\n    ):\n        self.llm = llm\n        self.wikidata_client = wikidata_api_client\n        self.concept_types = concept_types\n        self.max_concepts = max_concepts_per_chunk\n        \n    async def extract(self, text: str, existing_nodes: List[Any] = None) -> Tuple[List, List]:\n        \"\"\"\n        \u4ece\u6587\u672c\u4e2d\u62bd\u53d6\u9690\u6027\u6982\u5ff5\u3002\n        \n        Returns:\n            nodes: \u9690\u6027\u6982\u5ff5\u8282\u70b9\uff08\u5e26Wikidata Q\u53f7\uff09\n            relations: \u6982\u5ff5\u4e0e\u6587\u672c\u5757\u7684EMBODIES\u5173\u7cfb\n        \"\"\"\n        # Step 1: LLM\u8bc6\u522b\u9690\u6027\u6982\u5ff5\uff08\u81ea\u7136\u8bed\u8a00\uff09\n        prompt = self._build_licr_prompt(text)\n        response = await self.llm.acomplete(prompt)\n        implicit_concepts = self._parse_concepts(response)\n        \n        # Step 2: Wikidata API\u83b7\u53d6Q\u53f7\n        nodes = []\n        for concept in implicit_concepts:\n            q_id = await self.wikidata_client.search(concept[\"name\"])\n            if q_id:\n                nodes.append(EntityNode(\n                    name=concept[\"name\"],\n                    label=\"ImplicitConcept\",\n                    properties={\n                        \"wikidata_id\": q_id,\n                        \"hierarchy\": concept[\"hierarchy\"],\n                        \"evidence\": concept[\"evidence\"],\n                        \"extraction_type\": \"implicit\"\n                    }\n                ))\n        \n        # Step 3: \u521b\u5efaEMBODIES\u5173\u7cfb\n        relations = [\n            Relation(\n                source_id=text_chunk_id,\n                target_id=node.id,\n                label=\"EMBODIES\"\n            )\n            for node in nodes\n        ]\n        \n        return nodes, relations\n```\n\n### 3.2 \u4f5c\u4e3aLangChain Transformer\u96c6\u6210\n\n```python\n# \u63d0\u8bae\u7684LICR Graph Transformer\nfrom langchain_experimental.graph_transformers import LLMGraphTransformer\nfrom langchain_core.documents import Document\n\nclass LICRGraphTransformer(LLMGraphTransformer):\n    \"\"\"\n    \u6269\u5c55LLMGraphTransformer\uff0c\u6dfb\u52a0\u9690\u6027\u6982\u5ff5\u8bc6\u522b\u80fd\u529b\u3002\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        llm,\n        wikidata_api_url: str = \"https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php\",\n        extract_implicit: bool = True,\n        **kwargs\n    ):\n        super().__init__(llm=llm, **kwargs)\n        self.wikidata_api_url = wikidata_api_url\n        self.extract_implicit = extract_implicit\n        \n    def convert_to_graph_documents(self, documents: List[Document]):\n        # \u539f\u6709\u7684\u663e\u5f0f\u5b9e\u4f53\u62bd\u53d6\n        graph_docs = super().convert_to_graph_documents(documents)\n        \n        if self.extract_implicit:\n            # LICR: \u9690\u6027\u6982\u5ff5\u62bd\u53d6\n            for doc, graph_doc in zip(documents, graph_docs):\n                implicit_nodes = self._extract_implicit_concepts(doc.page_content)\n                graph_doc.nodes.extend(implicit_nodes)\n                \n                # \u6dfb\u52a0EMBODIES\u8fb9\n                for node in implicit_nodes:\n                    graph_doc.relationships.append(\n                        Relationship(\n                            source=doc,\n                            target=node,\n                            type=\"EMBODIES\"\n                        )\n                    )\n        \n        return graph_docs\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. \u5b9e\u73b0\u8def\u7ebf\u56fe\n\n### Phase 1: \u72ec\u7acb\u6a21\u5757\uff081-2\u4e2a\u6708\uff09\n\n```\n\u5f00\u53d1\u72ec\u7acb\u7684LICR Python\u5305\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 licr/\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 extractor.py      # \u6838\u5fc3\u62bd\u53d6\u903b\u8f91\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 wikidata.py       # Wikidata API\u5ba2\u6237\u7aef\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 prompts/          # \u591a\u9886\u57df\u63d0\u793a\u8bcd\u6a21\u677f\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 evaluation/       # \u8bc4\u4f30\u5de5\u5177\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 \u53d1\u5e03\u5230PyPI\n```\n\n### Phase 2: LlamaIndex\u96c6\u6210\uff081\u4e2a\u6708\uff09\n\n```\n\u8d21\u732e\u5230llama-index-integrations/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 llama-index-extractors-licr/\n\u2502   \u251c\u2500\u2500 LICRPathExtractor\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 \u6587\u6863\u548c\u793a\u4f8b\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 \u63d0\u4ea4PR\u5230LlamaIndex\u4ed3\u5e93\n```\n\n### Phase 3: LangChain\u96c6\u6210\uff081\u4e2a\u6708\uff09\n\n```\n\u8d21\u732e\u5230langchain-experimental/\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 graph_transformers/\n\u2502   \u2514\u2500\u2500 licr_transformer.py\n\u2514\u2500\u2500 \u63d0\u4ea4PR\u5230LangChain\u4ed3\u5e93\n```\n\n### Phase 4: GraphRAG\u96c6\u6210\uff082\u4e2a\u6708\uff09\n\n```\nFork microsoft/graphrag\n\u251c\u2500\u2500 text: str\\n    explicit_concepts: list[str]\\n    implicit_concepts: list[ImplicitConcept]\\n\\n\\n# ============================================================================\\n# Wikidata API SEARCH_URL = \\\"https://www.wikidata.org/w/api.php\\\"\\n    \\n    def search(self, concept_name: str, language: str = \\\"en\\\") -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Search Wikidata for a concept and return its Q-identifier.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            concept_name: The concept name to search for\\n            language: Language code (default: \\\"en\\\")\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            Q-identifier string (e.g., \\\"Q184899\\\") or None if not found\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        params = {\\n            \\\"action\\\": \\\"wbsearchentities\\\",\\n            \\\"search\\\": concept_name,\\n            \\\"language\\\": language,\\n            \\\"format\\\": \\\"json\\\",\\n            \\\"limit\\\": 1\\n        }\\n        \\n        try:\\n            response = requests.get(self.SEARCH_URL, params=params, timeout=10)\\n            response.raise_for_status()\\n            data = response.json()\\n            \\n            if data.get(\\\"search\\\"):\\n                return data[\\\"search\\\"][0][\\\"id\\\"]\\n            return None\\n            \\n        except requests.RequestException as e:\\n            print(f\\\"Wikidata API error: {e}\\\")\\n            return None\\n    \\n    def get_label(self, qid: str, language: str = \\\"en\\\") -> Optional[str]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Get the label for a Q-identifier.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        params = {\\n            \\\"action\\\": \\\"wbgetentities\\\",\\n            \\\"ids\\\": qid,\\n            \\\"props\\\": \\\"labels\\\",\\n            \\\"languages\\\": language,\\n            \\\"format\\\": \\\"json\\\"\\n        }\\n        \\n        try:\\n            response = requests.get(self.SEARCH_URL, params=params, timeout=10)\\n            response.raise_for_status()\\n            data = response.json()\\n            \\n            if \\\"entities\\\" in data and qid in data[\\\"entities\\\"]:\\n                labels = data[\\\"entities\\\"][qid].get(\\\"labels\\\", {})\\n                if language in labels:\\n                    return labels[language][\\\"value\\\"]\\n            return None\\n            \\n        except requests.RequestException:\\n            return None\\n\\n\\n# ============================================================================\\n# LLM Backend Interface\\n# @abstractmethod\\n    def complete(self, prompt: str) -> str:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Send prompt to LLM and return response.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        pass\\n\\n\\nclass OpenAIBackend(LLMBackend):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"OpenAI API backend.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    def __init__(self, api_key: str, model: str = \\\"gpt-4\\\"):\\n        self.api_key = api_key\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.api_url = \\\"https://api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions\\\"\\n    \\n    def complete(self, prompt: str) -> str:\\n        headers = {\\n            \\\"Authorization\\\": f\\\"Bearer {self.api_key}\\\",\\n            \\\"Content-Type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\"\\n        }\\n        data = {\\n            \\\"model\\\": self.model,\\n            \\\"messages\\\": [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}],\\n            \\\"temperature\\\": 0\\n        }\\n        \\n        response = requests.post(self.api_url, headers=headers, json=data, timeout=60)\\n        response.raise_for_status()\\n        return response.json()[\\\"choices\\\"][0][\\\"message\\\"][\\\"content\\\"]\\n\\n\\nclass AnthropicBackend(LLMBackend):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Anthropic API backend.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    def __init__(self, api_key: str, model: str = \\\"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\\\"):\\n        self.api_key = api_key\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.api_url = \\\"https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages\\\"\\n    \\n    def complete(self, prompt: str) -> str:\\n        headers = {\\n            \\\"x-api-key\\\": self.api_key,\\n            \\\"Content-Type\\\": \\\"application/json\\\",\\n            \\\"anthropic-version\\\": \\\"2023-06-01\\\"\\n        }\\n        data = {\\n            \\\"model\\\": self.model,\\n            \\\"max_tokens\\\": 4096,\\n            \\\"messages\\\": [{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}]\\n        }\\n        \\n        response = requests.post(self.api_url, headers=headers, json=data, timeout=60)\\n        response.raise_for_status()\\n        return response.json()[\\\"content\\\"][0][\\\"text\\\"]\\n\\n\\nclass MockBackend(LLMBackend):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Mock backend for testing without API calls.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    def __init__(self):\\n        # Pre-defined responses for test cases\\n        self.responses = {\\n            \\\"sqrt2\\\": json.dumps({\\n                \\\"explicit_concepts\\\": [\\\"square root of 2\\\", \\\"rational number\\\", \\\"coprime integers\\\", \\\"irrational number\\\"],\\n                \\\"implicit_concepts\\\": [\\n                    {\\\"concept\\\": \\\"proof by contradiction\\\", \\\"evidence\\\": \\\"Assume proposition, derive contradiction, negate assumption\\\", \\\"hierarchy\\\": \\\"methodology\\\"},\\n                    {\\\"concept\\\": \\\"reductio ad absurdum\\\", \\\"evidence\\\": \\\"Latin term for proof by contradiction\\\", \\\"hierarchy\\\": \\\"methodology\\\"},\\n                    {\\\"concept\\\": \\\"mathematical proof\\\", \\\"evidence\\\": \\\"Formal logical reasoning process\\\", \\\"hierarchy\\\": \\\"methodology\\\"}\\n                ]\\n            }),\\n            \\\"quicksort\\\": json.dumps({\\n                \\\"explicit_concepts\\\": [\\\"array\\\", \\\"pivot element\\\", \\\"partition\\\", \\\"recursion\\\", \\\"subarray\\\"],\\n                \\\"implicit_concepts\\\": [\\n                    {\\\"concept\\\": \\\"quicksort\\\", \\\"evidence\\\": \\\"Pivot selection, partitioning, recursive processing\\\", \\\"hierarchy\\\": \\\"domain\\\"},\\n                    {\\\"concept\\\": \\\"divide and conquer\\\", \\\"evidence\\\": \\\"Decompose problem into subproblems\\\", \\\"hierarchy\\\": \\\"methodology\\\"}\\n                ]\\n            })\\n        }\\n    \\n    def complete(self, prompt: str) -> str:\\n        # Return appropriate mock response based on prompt content\\n or \\\"partition\\\" in prompt.lower():\\n            return self.responses[\\\"quicksort\\\"]\\n        else:\\n            return json.dumps({\\\"explicit_concepts\\\": [], \\\"implicit_concepts\\\": []})\\n\\n\\n# ============================================================================\\n# LICR Core Engine\\n# ============================================================================\\n\\nclass LICR:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    LICR: LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition\\n    \\n    Main class implementing the hybrid architecture for implicit concept recognition.\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    \\n    PROMPT_TEMPLATE = '''You are a Wikidata concept annotation expert. Analyze the following text and identify all concepts linkable to Wikidata.\\n\\nRequirements:\\n1. Identify [Explicit Concepts]: Entities directly mentioned in text (names, places, terms, etc.)\\n2. Identify [Implicit Concepts]: Methods, principles, patterns that text embodies but does not explicitly name\\n   - This is the KEY task: identify what the text \\\"does\\\" rather than what it \\\"says\\\"\\n3. Use Wikidata standard English concept names\\n4. Classify by hierarchy:\\n   - methodology: Proof methods, analytical frameworks, research paradigms\\n   - domain: Disciplinary terms, theoretical models\\n   - entity: People, objects, events\\n\\nOutput ONLY valid JSON (no markdown, no explanation):\\n{{\\n  \\\"explicit_concepts\\\": [\\\"concept1\\\", \\\"concept2\\\", ...],\\n  \\\"implicit_concepts\\\": [\\n    {{\\n      \\\"concept\\\": \\\"concept name\\\",\\n      \\\"evidence\\\": \\\"specific text evidence supporting inference\\\",\\n      \\\"hierarchy\\\": \\\"methodology/domain/entity\\\"\\n    }}\\n  ]\\n}}\\n\\nText to analyze:\\n{text}'''\\n\\n    def __init__(self, llm_backend: LLMBackend, wikidata_client: Optional[WikidataClient] = None):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Initialize LICR engine.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            llm_backend: LLM backend for concept extraction\\n            wikidata_client: Wikidata client for Q-ID resolution (optional)\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        self.llm = llm_backend\\n        self.wikidata = wikidata_client or WikidataClient()\\n    \\n    def extract(self, text: str, resolve_wikidata: bool = True) -> LICRResult:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        Extract implicit concepts from text.\\n        \\n        Args:\\n            text: Input text to analyze\\n            resolve_wikidata: Whether to resolve Wikidata Q-identifiers\\n            \\n        Returns:\\n            LICRResult containing explicit and implicit concepts\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        # Step 1: LLM extracts concepts (semantic understanding)\\n        prompt = self.PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(text=text)\\n        response = self.llm.complete(prompt)\\n        \\n        # Step 2: Parse LLM response\\n        try:\\n            # Handle potential markdown code blocks\\n            if \\\"```json\\\" in response:\\n                response = response.split(\\\"```json\\\")[1].split(\\\"```\\\")[0]\\n            elif \\\"```\\\" in response:\\n                response = response.split(\\\"```\\\")[1].split(\\\"```\\\")[0]\\n            \\n            data = json.loads(response.strip())\\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\\n            print(f\\\"JSON parse error: {e}\\\")\\n            print(f\\\"Raw response: {response}\\\")\\n            return LICRResult(text=text, explicit_concepts=[], implicit_concepts=[])\\n        \\n        explicit = data.get(\\\"explicit_concepts\\\", [])\\n        \\n        # Step 3: Build ImplicitConcept objects\\n        implicit_concepts = []\\n        for item in data.get(\\\"implicit_concepts\\\", []):\\n            concept = ImplicitConcept(\\n                name=item.get(\\\"concept\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                evidence=item.get(\\\"evidence\\\", \\\"\\\"),\\n                hierarchy=item.get(\\\"hierarchy\\\", \\\"domain\\\")\\n            )\\n            \\n            # Step 4: Resolve Wikidata Q-identifiers (API precision)\\n            if resolve_wikidata:\\n                qid = self.wikidata.search(concept.name)\\n                if qid:\\n                    concept.wikidata_id = qid\\n                    concept.wikidata_label = self.wikidata.get_label(qid)\\n            \\n            implicit_concepts.append(concept)\\n        \\n        return LICRResult(\\n            text=text,\\n            explicit_concepts=explicit,\\n            implicit_concepts=implicit_concepts\\n        )\\n    \\n    def to_dict(self, result: LICRResult) -> dict:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Convert LICRResult to dictionary for JSON serialization.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        return {\\n            \\\"text\\\": result.text,\\n            \\\"explicit_concepts\\\": result.explicit_concepts,\\n            \\\"implicit_concepts\\\": [\\n                {\\n                    \\\"name\\\": c.name,\\n                    \\\"evidence\\\": c.evidence,\\n                    \\\"hierarchy\\\": c.hierarchy,\\n                    \\\"wikidata_id\\\": c.wikidata_id,\\n                    \\\"wikidata_label\\\": c.wikidata_label\\n                }\\n                for c in result.implicit_concepts\\n            ]\\n        }\\n\\n\\n# ============================================================================\\n# Test Cases from Paper\\n# ============================================================================\\n\\nTEST_CASES = {\\n    \\\"math_proof\\\": {\\n        \\\"text\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"Suppose \u221a2 = p/q where p and q are coprime. Then 2q\u00b2 = p\u00b2, so p\u00b2 is even, \\nthus p is even. Let p = 2k, then q\u00b2 = 2k\u00b2, so q is also even. But this \\ncontradicts our assumption that p and q are coprime. Therefore \u221a2 is irrational.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n        \\\"expected_implicit\\\": [\\\"proof by contradiction\\\", \\\"reductio ad absurdum\\\", \\\"mathematical proof\\\"]\\n    },\\n    \\n    \\\"algorithm\\\": {\\n        \\\"text\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"To sort an array, pick a pivot element. Partition the array so elements \\nless than pivot go left, greater go right. Then recursively apply the \\nsame process to the left and right subarrays until each subarray has \\none element.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n        \\\"expected_implicit\\\": [\\\"quicksort\\\", \\\"divide and conquer\\\", \\\"recursion\\\"]\\n    },\\n    \\n    \\\"economics\\\": {\\n        \\\"text\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"Workers demanded higher wages because prices kept rising. Companies raised \\nprices to cover increased labor costs. This led workers to demand even \\nhigher wages. The cycle continued, with each round of increases triggering \\nthe next.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n        \\\"expected_implicit\\\": [\\\"wage-price spiral\\\", \\\"inflation\\\", \\\"positive feedback loop\\\"]\\n    },\\n    \\n    \\\"game_theory\\\": {\\n        \\\"text\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"Two suspects are interrogated separately. If both stay silent, each gets \\n1 year. If one confesses while the other stays silent, the confessor goes \\nfree while the other gets 10 years. If both confess, each gets 5 years. \\nRationally, each suspect finds that confessing is better regardless of \\nwhat the other does.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n        \\\"expected_implicit\\\": [\\\"prisoner's dilemma\\\", \\\"game theory\\\", \\\"Nash equilibrium\\\", \\\"dominant strategy\\\"]\\n    },\\n    \\n    \\\"physics\\\": {\\n        \\\"text\\\": \\\"\\\"\\\"A beam of light passes through two narrow parallel slits. On the screen \\nbehind, instead of two bright lines, we observe alternating bright and \\ndark bands. The pattern changes when we try to detect which slit each \\nphoton passes through.\\\"\\\"\\\",\\n        \\\"expected_implicit\\\": [\\\"double-slit experiment\\\", \\\"wave interference\\\", \\\"wave-particle duality\\\"]\\n    }\\n}\\n\\n\\n# ============================================================================\\n# CLI Interface\\n# ============================================================================\\n\\ndef main():\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Command-line interface for LICR.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    import argparse\\n    \\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\\n        description=\\\"LICR: LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition\\\",\\n        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,\\n        epilog=\\\"\\\"\\\"\\nExamples:\\n  #  \"implicit_concepts\": [\n                    {\"concept\": \"quicksort\", \"evidence\": \"Pivot selection, partitioning, recursive processing\", \"hierarchy\": \"domain\"},\n                    {\"concept\": \"divide and conquer\", \"evidence\": \"Decompose problem into subproblems\", \"hierarchy\": \"methodology\"}\n                ]\n            })\n        }\n    \n    def complete(self, prompt: str) -> str:\n        # Return appropriate mock response based on prompt content\n        if \"\u221a2\" in prompt or \"sqrt\" in prompt.lower() or \"coprime\" in prompt.lower():\n            return self.responses[\"sqrt2\"]\n        elif \"pivot\" in prompt.lower() or \"partition\" in prompt.lower():\n            return self.responses[\"quicksort\"]\n        else:\n            return json.dumps({\"explicit_concepts\": [], \"implicit_concepts\": []})\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# LICR Core Engine\n# ============================================================================\n\nclass LICR:\n    \"\"\"\n    LICR: LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition\n    \n    Main class implementing the hybrid architecture for implicit concept recognition.\n    \"\"\"\n    \n    PROMPT_TEMPLATE = '''You are a Wikidata concept annotation expert. Analyze the following text and identify all concepts linkable to Wikidata.\n\nRequirements:\n1. Identify [Explicit Concepts]: Entities directly mentioned in text (names, places, terms, etc.)\n2. Identify [Implicit Concepts]: Methods, principles, patterns that text embodies but does not explicitly name\n   - This is the KEY task: identify what the text \"does\" rather than what it \"says\"\n3. Use Wikidata standard English concept names\n4. Classify by hierarchy:\n   - methodology: Proof methods, analytical frameworks, research paradigms\n   - domain: Disciplinary terms, theoretical models\n   - entity: People, objects, events\n\nOutput ONLY valid JSON (no markdown, no explanation):\n{{\n  \"explicit_concepts\": [\"concept1\", \"concept2\", ...],\n  \"implicit_concepts\": [\n    {{\n      \"concept\": \"concept name\",\n      \"evidence\": \"specific text evidence supporting inference\",\n      \"hierarchy\": \"methodology/domain/entity\"\n    }}\n  ]\n}}\n\nText to analyze:\n{text}'''\n\n    def __init__(self, llm_backend: LLMBackend, wikidata_client: Optional[WikidataClient] = None):\n        \"\"\"\n        Initialize LICR engine.\n        \n        Args:\n            llm_backend: LLM backend for concept extraction\n            wikidata_client: Wikidata client for Q-ID resolution (optional)\n        \"\"\"\n        self.llm = llm_backend\n        self.wikidata = wikidata_client or WikidataClient()\n    \n    def extract(self, text: str, resolve_wikidata: bool = True) -> LICRResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Extract implicit concepts from text.\n        \n        Args:\n            text: Input text to analyze\n            resolve_wikidata: Whether to resolve Wikidata Q-identifiers\n            \n        Returns:\n            LICRResult containing explicit and implicit concepts\n        \"\"\"\n        # Step 1: LLM extracts concepts (semantic understanding)\n        prompt = self.PROMPT_TEMPLATE.format(text=text)\n        response = self.llm.complete(prompt)\n        \n        # Step 2: Parse LLM response\n        try:\n            # Handle potential markdown code blocks\n            if \"```json\" in response:\n                response = response.split(\"```json\")[1].split(\"```\")[0]\n            elif \"```\" in response:\n                response = response.split(\"```\")[1].split(\"```\")[0]\n            \n            data = json.loads(response.strip())\n        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:\n            print(f\"JSON parse error: {e}\")\n            print(f\"Raw response: {response}\")\n            return LICRResult(text=text, explicit_concepts=[], implicit_concepts=[])\n        \n        explicit = data.get(\"explicit_concepts\", [])\n        \n        # Step 3: Build ImplicitConcept objects\n        implicit_concepts = []\n        for item in data.get(\"implicit_concepts\", []):\n            concept = ImplicitConcept(\n                name=item.get(\"concept\", \"\"),\n                evidence=item.get(\"evidence\", \"\"),\n                hierarchy=item.get(\"hierarchy\", \"domain\")\n            )\n            \n            # Step 4: Resolve Wikidata Q-identifiers (API precision)\n            if resolve_wikidata:\n                qid = self.wikidata.search(concept.name)\n                if qid:\n                    concept.wikidata_id = qid\n                    concept.wikidata_label = self.wikidata.get_label(qid)\n            \n            implicit_concepts.append(concept)\n        \n        return LICRResult(\n            text=text,\n            explicit_concepts=explicit,\n            implicit_concepts=implicit_concepts\n        )\n    \n    def to_dict(self, result: LICRResult) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Convert LICRResult to dictionary for JSON serialization.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"text\": result.text,\n            \"explicit_concepts\": result.explicit_concepts,\n            \"implicit_concepts\": [\n                {\n                    \"name\": c.name,\n                    \"evidence\": c.evidence,\n                    \"hierarchy\": c.hierarchy,\n                    \"wikidata_id\": c.wikidata_id,\n                    \"wikidata_label\": c.wikidata_label\n                }\n                for c in result.implicit_concepts\n            ]\n        }\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Test Cases from Paper\n# ============================================================================\n\nTEST_CASES = {\n    \"math_proof\": {\n        \"text\": \"\"\"Suppose \u221a2 = p/q where p and q are coprime. Then 2q\u00b2 = p\u00b2, so p\u00b2 is even, \nthus p is even. Let p = 2k, then q\u00b2 = 2k\u00b2, so q is also even. But this \ncontradicts our assumption that p and q are coprime. Therefore \u221a2 is irrational.\"\"\",\n        \"expected_implicit\": [\"proof by contradiction\", \"reductio ad absurdum\", \"mathematical proof\"]\n    },\n    \n    \"algorithm\": {\n        \"text\": \"\"\"To sort an array, pick a pivot element. Partition the array so elements \nless than pivot go left, greater go right. Then recursively apply the \nsame process to the left and right subarrays until each subarray has \none element.\"\"\",\n        \"expected_implicit\": [\"quicksort\", \"divide and conquer\", \"recursion\"]\n    },\n    \n    \"economics\": {\n        \"text\": \"\"\"Workers demanded higher wages because prices kept rising. Companies raised \nprices to cover increased labor costs. This led workers to demand even \nhigher wages. The cycle continued, with each round of increases triggering \nthe next.\"\"\",\n        \"expected_implicit\": [\"wage-price spiral\", \"inflation\", \"positive feedback loop\"]\n    },\n    \n    \"game_theory\": {\n        \"text\": \"\"\"Two suspects are interrogated separately. If both stay silent, each gets \n1 year. If one confesses while the other stays silent, the confessor goes \nfree while the other gets 10 years. If both confess, each gets 5 years. \nRationally, each suspect finds that confessing is better regardless of \nwhat the other does.\"\"\",\n        \"expected_implicit\": [\"prisoner's dilemma\", \"game theory\", \"Nash equilibrium\", \"dominant strategy\"]\n    },\n    \n    \"physics\": {\n        \"text\": \"\"\"A beam of light passes through two narrow parallel slits. On the screen \nbehind, instead of two bright lines, we observe alternating bright and \ndark bands. The pattern changes when we try to detect which slit each \nphoton passes through.\"\"\",\n        \"expected_implicit\": [\"double-slit experiment\", \"wave interference\", \"wave-particle duality\"]\n    }\n}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# CLI Interface\n# ============================================================================\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Command-line interface for LICR.\"\"\"\n    import argparse\n    \n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"LICR: LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition\",\n        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,\n        epilog=\"\"\"\nExamples:\n  # Run with mock backend (no API key needed)\n  python licr.py --mock --test math_proof\n  \n  # Run with OpenAI\n  python licr.py --openai YOUR_API_KEY --text \"Your text here\"\n  \n  # Run with Anthropic\n  python licr.py --anthropic YOUR_API_KEY --test algorithm\n  \n  # Run all test cases\n  python licr.py --mock --test all\n        \"\"\"\n    )\n    \n    # Backend selection\n    backend_group = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)\n    backend_group.add_argument(\"--mock\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Use mock backend (for testing)\")\n    backend_group.add_argument(\"--openai\", metavar=\"API_KEY\", help=\"Use OpenAI backend\")\n    backend_group.add_argument(\"--anthropic\", metavar=\"API_KEY\", help=\"Use Anthropic backend\")\n    \n    # Input selection\n    input_group = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)\n    input_group.add_argument(\"--text\", help=\"Text to analyze\")\n    input_group.add_argument(\"--test\", choices=list(TEST_CASES.keys()) + [\"all\"], help=\"Run test case\")\n    \n    # Options\n    parser.add_argument(\"--no-wikidata\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Skip Wikidata resolution\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--json\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Output as JSON\")\n    \n    args = parser.parse_args()\n    \n    # Initialize backend\n    if args.mock:\n        backend = MockBackend()\n    elif args.openai:\n        backend = OpenAIBackend(args.openai)\n    else:\n        backend = AnthropicBackend(args.anthropic)\n    \n    # Initialize LICR\n    licr = LICR(backend)\n    \n    # Process input\n    if args.text:\n        texts = [(\"custom\", args.text)]\n    elif args.test == \"all\":\n        texts = [(name, case[\"text\"]) for name, case in TEST_CASES.items()]\n    else:\n        texts = [(args.test, TEST_CASES[args.test][\"text\"])]\n    \n    # Run extraction\n    for name, text in texts:\n        print(f\"\\n{'='*60}\")\n        print(f\"Test Case: {name}\")\n        print(f\"{'='*60}\")\n        \n        result = licr.extract(text, resolve_wikidata=not args.no_wikidata)\n        \n        if args.json:\n            print(json.dumps(licr.to_dict(result), indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))\n        else:\n            print(f\"\\nInput Text:\\n{text[:200]}...\")\n            print(f\"\\nExplicit Concepts: {result.explicit_concepts}\")\n            print(f\"\\nImplicit Concepts:\")\n            for c in result.implicit_concepts:\n                qid_str = f\" [{c.wikidata_id}]\" if c.wikidata_id else \"\"\n                print(f\"  \u2022 {c.name}{qid_str}\")\n                print(f\"    Hierarchy: {c.hierarchy}\")\n                print(f\"    Evidence: {c.evidence}\")\n                if c.wikidata_label:\n                    print(f\"    Wikidata Label: {c.wikidata_label}\")\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"

LICR goes further by recognizing **implicit concepts**\u2014methodologies, principles, and patterns that texts embody but do not explicitly name.\\n\\n### Example\\n\\nGiven this mathematical proof:\\n\\n> \\\"Suppose \u221a2 = p/q where p and q are coprime. Then 2q\u00b2 = p\u00b2, so p\u00b2 is even, thus p is even. Let p = 2k, then q\u00b2 = 2k\u00b2, so q is also even. But this contradicts our assumption. Concept name\\n    evidence: str                # Text evidence\\n    hierarchy: str               # methodology / domain / entity\\n    wikidata_id: Optional[str]   # Q-identifier\\n    wikidata_label: Optional[str]\\n\\n@dataclass  \\nclass LICRResult:\\n    text: str\\n    explicit_concepts: list[str]\\n    implicit_concepts: list[ImplicitConcept]\\n```\\n\\n### LLM Backends\\n\\n- `MockBackend()` \u2014 For testing without API calls\\n- `OpenAIBackend(api_key, model=\\\"gpt-4\\\")` \u2014 OpenAI API\\n- `AnthropicBackend(api_key, model=\\\"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\\\")` \u2014 Anthropic API\\n\\n## Integration with RAG Frameworks\\n\\nLICR is designed to integrate with existing knowledge graph frameworks:\\n\\n- **LlamaIndex**: As a custom `PathExtractor`\\n- **LangChain**: As an extension to `LLMGraphTransformer`\\n- **Microsoft GraphRAG**: As an entity extraction enhancement\\n\\nSee the [integration analysis](LICR_integration_analysis.md) for detailed architecture proposals.\\n\\n## Citation\\n\\nIf you use LICR in your research, please cite:\\n\\n```bibtex\\n@misc{licr2025,\\n  title={Beyond Named Entities: LICR - An Empirical Study of LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition},\\n  author={Independent Researcher},\\n  year={2025},\\n  publisher={Zenodo},\\n  doi={10.5281/zenodo.XXXXXXX}\\n}\\n```\\n\\n## License\\n\\nThis project is licensed under [CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/).\\n\\n## Prior Art Notice\\n\\nThis repository establishes prior art for the LICR methodology and architecture, preventing patent claims on:\\n- Implicit concept recognition using LLMs\\n- Hybrid LLM-API architecture for knowledge base linking\\n- The specific prompt engineering techniques described herein\\n\",\"stderr\":\"\"}",

Therefore \u221a2 is irrational.\\\"\\n\\n**Traditional Entity Linking finds:** \u221a2, rational number, coprime, irrational number\\n\\n**LICR additionally finds:**\\n- `proof by contradiction` (Q184899) \u2014 the proof method used\\n- `reductio ad absurdum` (Q184899) \u2014 Latin synonym  \\n- `mathematical proof` (Q11538) \u2014 the general methodology\\n\\n## Architecture\\n\\n```\\nText \u2192 LLM (semantic recognition) \u2192 Concept Names \u2192 Wikidata API \u2192 Q-identifiers\\n```\\n\\nLICR combines:\\n- **LLM's strength**: Semantic pattern recognition (100% recall on core concepts)\\n- **API's strength**: Precise identifier lookup (arbitrary Q-numbers)\\n\\n## Installation\\n\\n```bash\\npip install -r requirements.txt\\n```\\n\\n## Quick Start\\n\\n### Using Mock Backend (No API Key Required)\\n\\n```bash\\n# Run a single test case\\npython licr.py --mock --test math_proof\\n\\n# Run all test cases\\npython licr.py --mock --test all\\n```\\n\\n### Using OpenAI\\n\\n```bash\\npython licr.py --openai YOUR_API_KEY --text \\\"Your text to analyze\\\"\\n```\\n\\n### Using Anthropic Claude\\n\\n```bash\\npython licr.py --anthropic YOUR_API_KEY --test algorithm\\n```\\n\\n### As a Python Library\\n\\n```python\\nfrom licr import LICR, MockBackend, OpenAIBackend, WikidataClient\\n\\n# Initialize with mock backend (for testing)\\nbackend = MockBackend()\\nlicr = LICR(backend)\\n\\n# Or with real LLM\\n# backend = OpenAIBackend(\\\"your-api-key\\\")\\n# licr = LICR(backend)\\n\\n# Extract implicit concepts\\ntext = \\\"\\\"\\\"\\nSuppose \u221a2 = p/q where p and q are coprime. Then 2q\u00b2 = p\u00b2, \\nso p\u00b2 is even, thus p is even. Let p = 2k, then q\u00b2 = 2k\u00b2, \\nso q is also even. But this contradicts our assumption. \\nTherefore \u221a2 is irrational.\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nresult = licr.extract(text)\\n\\n# Access results\\nprint(\\\"Explicit concepts:\\\", result.explicit_concepts)\\nfor concept in result.implicit_concepts:\\n    print(f\\\"Implicit: {concept.name} [{concept.wikidata_id}]\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Evidence: {concept.evidence}\\\")\\n```\\n\\n## Test Cases\\n\\nLICR includes 5 cross-domain test cases from the research paper:\\n\\n| Test Case | Domain | Key Implicit Concepts |\\n|-----------|--------|----------------------|\\n| `math_proof` | Mathematics | proof by contradiction, reductio ad absurdum |\\n| `algorithm` | Computer Science | quicksort, divide and conquer |\\n| `economics` | Economics | wage-price spiral, inflation |\\n| `game_theory` | Game Theory | prisoner's dilemma, Nash equilibrium |\\n| `physics` | Quantum Physics | double-slit experiment, wave-particle duality |\\n\\n## Research Results\\n\\nFrom our empirical study across 5 domains:\\n\\n- **Core Implicit Concept Recall**: 100%\\n- **Overall Accuracy**: 96%\\n- **LLM Q-Number Reliability**: Low (hence the hybrid architecture)\\n\\n## API Reference\\n\\n### LICR Class\\n\\n```python\\nclass LICR:\\n    def __init__(self, llm_backend: LLMBackend, wikidata_client: WikidataClient = None)\\n    def extract(self, text: str, resolve_wikidata: bool = True) -> LICRResult\\n    def to_dict(self, result: LICRResult) -> dict\\n```\\n\\n### Data Classes\\n\\n```python\\n@dataclass\\nclass ImplicitConcept:\\n    name: str                    # Concept name\\n    evidence: str                # Text evidence\\n    hierarchy: str               # methodology / domain / entity\\n    wikidata_id: Optional[str]   # Q-identifier\\n    wikidata_label: Optional[str]\\n\\n@dataclass  \\nclass LICRResult:\\n    text: str\\n    explicit_concepts: list[str]\\n    implicit_concepts: list[ImplicitConcept]\\n```\\n\\n### LLM Backends\\n\\n- `MockBackend()` \u2014 For testing without API calls\\n- `OpenAIBackend(api_key, model=\\\"gpt-4\\\")` \u2014 OpenAI API\\n- `AnthropicBackend(api_key, model=\\\"claude-sonnet-4-20250514\\\")` \u2014 Anthropic API\\n\\n## Integration with RAG Frameworks\\n\\nLICR is designed to integrate with existing knowledge graph frameworks:\\n\\n- **LlamaIndex**: As a custom `PathExtractor`\\n- **LangChain**: As an extension to `LLMGraphTransformer`\\n- **Microsoft GraphRAG**: As an entity extraction enhancement\\n\\nSee the [integration analysis](LICR_integration_analysis.md) for detailed architecture proposals.\\n\\n## Citation\\n\\nIf you use LICR in your research, please cite:\\n\\n```bibtex\\n@misc{licr2025,\\n  title={Beyond Named Entities: LICR - An Empirical Study of LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition},\\n  author={Independent Researcher},\\n  year={2025},\\n  publisher={Zenodo},\\n  doi={10.5281/zenodo.XXXXXXX}\\n}\\n```\\n\\n## License\\n\\nThis project is licensed under [CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/).\\n\\n## Prior Art Notice\\n\\nThis repository establishes prior art for the LICR methodology and architecture, preventing patent claims on:\\n- Implicit concept recognition using LLMs\\n- Hybrid LLM-API architecture for knowledge base linking\\n- The specific prompt engineering techniques described herein\\n\",\"stderr\":\"\"}"

      "content": "# LICR: LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition\n\n[![License: CC BY 4.0](https://img.shields.io/badge/License-CC%20BY%204.0-lightgrey.svg)](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/)\n[![Python 3.8+](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.8+-blue.svg)](https://www.python.org/downloads/)\n[![DOI](https://zenodo.org/badge/DOI/10.5281/zenodo.18211198.svg)](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18211198)\n\n**LICR** is a hybrid architecture for recognizing implicit concepts in text and linking them to Wikidata knowledge base.\n\n## What is Implicit Concept Recognition?\n\nTraditional entity linking systems can only identify **explicitly mentioned** entities. LICR goes further by recognizing **implicit concepts**\u2014methodologies, principles, and patterns that texts embody but do not explicitly name.\n\n### Example\n\nGiven this mathematical proof:\n\n> \"Suppose \u221a2 = p/q where p and q are coprime. Then 2q\u00b2 = p\u00b2, so p\u00b2 is even, thus p is even. Let p = 2k, then q\u00b2 = 2k\u00b2, so q is also even. But this contradicts our assumption. Therefore \u221a2 is irrational.\"\n\n**Traditional Entity Linking finds:** \u221a2, rational number, coprime, irrational number\n\n**LICR additionally finds:**\n- `proof by contradiction` (Q184899) \u2014 the proof method used\n- `reductio ad absurdum` (Q184899) \u2014 Latin synonym  \n- `mathematical proof` (Q11538) \u2014 the general methodology\n\n## Architecture\n\n```\nText \u2192 LLM (semantic recognition) \u2192 Concept Names \u2192 Wikidata API \u2192 Q-identifiers\n```\n\nLICR combines:\n- **LLM's strength**: Semantic pattern recognition (100% recall on core concepts)\n- **API's strength**: Precise identifier lookup (arbitrary Q-numbers)\n\n## Installation\n\n```bash\npip install -r requirements.txt\n```\n\n## Quick Start\n\n### Using Mock Backend (No API Key Required)\n\n```bash\n# Run a single test case\npython licr.py --mock --test math_proof\n\n# Run all test cases\npython licr.py --mock --test all\n```\n\n### Using OpenAI\n\n```bash\npython licr.py --openai YOUR_API_KEY --text \"Your text to analyze\"\n```\n\n### Using Anthropic Claude\n\n```bash\npython licr.py --anthropic YOUR_API_KEY --test algorithm\n```\n\n### As a Python Library\n\n```python\nfrom licr import LICR, MockBackend, OpenAIBackend, WikidataClient\n\n# Initialize with mock backend (for testing)\nbackend = MockBackend()\nlicr = LICR(backend)\n\n# Or with real LLM\n# backend = OpenAIBackend(\"your-api-key\")\n# licr = LICR(backend)\n\n# Extract implicit concepts\ntext = \"\"\"\nSuppose \u221a2 = p/q where p and q are coprime. Then 2q\u00b2 = p\u00b2, \nso p\u00b2 is even, thus p is even. Let p = 2k, then q\u00b2 = 2k\u00b2, \nso q is also even. But this contradicts our assumption. \nTherefore \u221a2 is irrational.\n\"\"\"\n\nresult = licr.extract(text)\n\n# Access results\nprint(\"Explicit concepts:\", result.explicit_concepts)\nfor concept in result.implicit_concepts:\n    print(f\"Implicit: {concept.name} [{concept.wikidata_id}]\")\n    print(f\"  Evidence: {concept.evidence}\")\n```\n\n## Test Cases\n\nLICR includes 5 cross-domain test cases from the research paper:\n\n| Test Case | Domain | Key Implicit Concepts |\n|-----------|--------|----------------------|\n| `math_proof` | Mathematics | proof by contradiction, reductio ad absurdum |\n| `algorithm` | Computer Science | quicksort, divide and conquer |\n| `economics` | Economics | wage-price spiral, inflation |\n| `game_theory` | Game Theory | prisoner's dilemma, Nash equilibrium |\n| `physics` | Quantum Physics | double-slit experiment, wave-particle duality |\n\n## Research Results\n\nFrom our empirical study across 5 domains:\n\n- **Core Implicit Concept Recall**: 100%\n- **Overall Accuracy**: 96%\n- **LLM Q-Number Reliability**: Low (hence the hybrid architecture)\n\n## Citation\n\nIf you use LICR in your research, please cite:\n\n```bibtex\n@misc{liu_claude_2025_licr,\n  title={Beyond Named Entities: LICR - An Empirical Study of LLM-based Implicit Concept Recognition},\n  author={Liu, Qizhuang and Claude, Opus 4.5},\n  year={2025},\n  publisher={Zenodo},\n  doi={10.5281/zenodo.18211198},\n  url={https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.18211198}\n}\n```\n\n## License\n\nThis project is licensed under [CC BY 4.0](https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/4.0/).\n\n## Prior Art Notice\n\nThis repository establishes prior art for the LICR methodology and architecture, preventing patent claims on:\n- Implicit concept recognition using LLMs\n- Hybrid LLM-API architecture for knowledge base linking\n- The specific prompt engineering techniques described herein\n\n## Authors\n\n- Liu, Qizhuang (Independent Researcher)\n- Claude, Opus 4.5 (Anthropic)\n"
